# Changelog

All notable changes to this project will be documented in this file.

The format is based on [Keep a Changelog](https://keepachangelog.com/en/1.0.0/),
and this project adheres to [Semantic Versioning](https://semver.org/spec/v2.0.0.html).

---

## [3.1.3] - 2026-11-08

Highlights: feat(infra): move the apex to the site and free the root from the docs proxy and fix(infra): drop the unsupported Pages API pagination query, plus 11 more changes.

- 🐛 fix(infra): drop the unsupported Pages API pagination query `cda644d`
  - `.github/workflows/infra-domain.yml`
- 🐛 fix(infra): send Content-Type only on the POST request `4b3fa75`
  - `.github/workflows/infra-domain.yml`
- 🐛 fix(infra): trim credential whitespace and log the Pages API response `d638610`
  - `.github/workflows/infra-domain.yml`
- 🐛 fix(infra): drop the redundant --fail flag `26ba2f1`
  - `.github/workflows/infra-domain.yml`
- 🐛 fix(infra): use valid Pages API pagination and surface error bodies `7f3e2ee`
  - `.github/workflows/infra-domain.yml`
- 🚀 feat(infra): move the apex to the site and free the root from the docs proxy `9ccd0ad`
  - `.github/workflows/infra-domain.yml`
  - `AGENTS.md`
  - `scripts/docs.mjs`
  - `web/public/_redirects`
- 📝 docs(readme): add the native URL map and fix the docs links `a922121`
  - `README.md`
- 🤖 ci(deploy): create the Pages project on first deploy `e00ccf7`
  - `.github/workflows/deploy.yml`
- 📝 docs(agents): document the Obsidian editing workflow `c80b841`
  - `AGENTS.md`
- 🤖 ci(deploy): fail fast when Cloudflare credentials are missing `0f014b8`
  - `.github/workflows/deploy.yml`
- 🚀 feat(i18n): default to English and localise all site content `4ab4cf1`
